        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "In TerraFirmaCraft, no food will last forever! Food will expire over time, turning rotten. Rotten food will not restore any hunger, and has the potential to give you unhelpful effects such as Hunger or Poison! Fortunately, there are a number of ways to make your food last longer by Preserving it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "When you hover over a piece of food, you will see a tooltip which shows how long the food has until it will rot. It might look something like: Expires on: 5:30 July 5, 1000 (in 5 day(s)) By using various preservation mechanics, that date can be extended, giving you more time before your food rots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "One of the easiest ways to preserve food is to use a Vessel. Large Vessels are a block which can store up to nine items, and when sealed the items inside will gain the Preserved status, which extends their remaining lifetime by 2x. Small Vessels are a item which can store up to four other items, and will also apply the Preserved status to their contents.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "A Sealed Large Vessel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "One other way to easily preserve certain types of food is to cook them. Meats will all expire slower when they are cooked than when they are raw. It is also important to use the correct device for cooking. Certain devices that heat very hot, such as a Charcoal Forge or a Crucible are bad for cooking food, which will make them expire faster!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "Instead, a Firepit or a Grill can even provide a buff for using it! For example, cooking mutton (pictured above) in a Firepit will increase its lifetime by 1.33x, and cooking in a Grill will increase its lifetime by 1.66x!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "Salting is a way to make meat last longer. To salt meat, it must be crafted with Salt in a crafting grid. Only raw meat can be salted. Afterwards, cooking or otherwise preserving the meat does not take away the salted property.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "One way of getting salt is through grinding Halite, which is a mineral.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "Salt Licks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.name",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "Salt can be found naturally in forests. It can be placed and picked back up.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "Vinegar Preservation is a way of making fruits, veggies, and meat last longer. Vinegar is made in a Barrel, by sealing a fruit with 250mB of Alcohol. To preserve food in vinegar it must first be Pickled in Brine. Brine is made in a barrel with 1 part Vinegar and 9 parts Salt Water.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "Once food is pickled, it can be sealed in a Barrel of Vinegar. If there is 125mB of Vinegar per pickled food item, the food will last longer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/decay.json",
          "text": "Food should be stored in either sealed large vessels or in Small Vessels. Food that is left sitting in chests or other devices can attract Rats. Rats are able to open containers and eat the food out of them, but will despawn if they can't find something for a few minutes. You will be alerted to a rat's presence by the message 'This container has a foul smell'. It is important to note that unsealing and resealing a large vessel before you leave the screen will never attract pests.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Preservation",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:world/eat_rotten_food",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/world/eat_rotten_food.json",
          "text": "Desperation",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "Desperation",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "tfc:world/eat_rotten_food",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!data/tfc/advancements/world/eat_rotten_food.json",
          "text": "Eat something rotten",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "Desperation",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:rotten_flesh",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "0.5 / 16.0g.",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Rotten Flesh",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:rotten_flesh",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Expires on: 12:00 June 1, 1000 (in 00:03)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Rotten Flesh",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:rotten_flesh",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Hunger (00:30)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Rotten Flesh",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Ghost Peppers (Sorrow) are an edible vegetable that can be grown in the Beneath!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Curious Blocks",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Soul Clay",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.name",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Soul Clay may be discovered in soul sand valleys. It is a source of regular clay.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Sulfur can be broken with a shovel. Be careful, though: iron-containing items (including steel) explode in contact with sulfur!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Cutting grass will yield you warped or crimson straw. This can be made into thatch, the normal way.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "The blackstone aqueduct allows lava to be conducted at any distance.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Hellbricks are a building block that is also used for the Hellforge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Blackstone bricks are made like regular bricks, and that includes needing to create the brick item.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge is a gratuitously large forge, with high capacity for fuel, products, and heating. It is constructed from Hellbricks, Anthracite, and Pyroxenite Aqueducts.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge requires lava to flow in aqueducts in its four corners, as well as 21 hellbricks. The 3x3 center must be filled with Anthracite piles, which then may be lit to start the forge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The item slots of the Hellforge may contain fuel, or things to be smelted; there is no distinction. Be careful what you put in! There are also slots for melting things, as with the charcoal forge. The lava meter on the screen indicates temperature (as well as the visual movement of its items in world).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge is a gratuitously large forge, with high capacity for fuel, products, and heating. It is constructed from Hellbricks, Cursecoal, and Blackstone Aqueducts.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge requires lava to flow in aqueducts in its four corners, as well as 21 hellbricks. The 3x3 center must be filled with Cursecoal piles, which then may be lit to start the forge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The item slots of the Hellforge may contain fuel, or things to be smelted; there is no distinction. Be careful what you put in! There are also slots for melting things, as with the charcoal forge. The lava meter on the screen indicates temperature (as well as the visual movement of its items in world).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The ores you can find Beneath are mostly similar to what you can find on the surface, but with a few changes. Consult the Ores in TFG category for exact details.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The most common rock type found is Migmatite. This deep metamorphic rock contains a wide variety of ores you'd normally find in other rock types on the surface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "In wetter areas, you may find Travertine. It is a sedimentary rock that can contain the same kind of ores as Migmatite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Around the deepest level of the Beneath, the different rock types gradually all turn into Pyroxenite. This ultramafic igneous rock is extremely rich in ores, but this depth also comes with extreme heat, so be prepared!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Anthracite is a powerful fuel. It can be found in Migmatite in the upper half of the Beneath.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Beneath the overworld, you will still find ore veins to exploit. It is important to note that Netherrack does not collapse, like raw stone would. However, Blackstone, Nether Bricks, Cobblerack and miscellaneous other blocks do. Prospecting will still work Beneath the overworld.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold replaces netherrack, and is common. Beneath lava, there is more gold, and richer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Cursecoal is a powerful fuel. It replaces netherrack above level 80.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Sylvite replaces basalt at any level.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz replaces netherrack at all levels, and is common.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Slimed Netherrack",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Slimed Netherrack occasionally replaces Netherrack above lava level.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "The Dugout Canoe will likely be the first step on your aquatic journey! If you're in the copper age, you can probably build a canoe.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "You can put blocks or animals in the pilot seat, but be aware that there's no way to paddle the boat if you do this! It's most useful if you just want to send materials down a river on their own. A canoe has two seats, and is piloted from the rear. You need to hold a canoe paddle in order to move at full speed, and if two players are holding paddles, you can go even faster.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "To build a Dugout Canoe, place 3 stripped logs in a row, horizontally, and cut off the tops with a saw. Then, carve out the canoe shape with an axe. Finally, light the inside of the canoe on fire to hollow out the hull. It will take the same amount of time as a pit kiln to complete the hollowing process.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "Carving out a Dugout Canoe requires a softer wood, and as a result, only some logs are workable: Douglas Fir Pine Palm Spruce Sequoia Kapok Mangrove White Cedar Willow",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/canoe.json",
          "text": "Just a Log",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.name",
          "label": "Dugout Canoes",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "The Beneath hosts its own unique selection of wild fruits, which have adapted to the extreme conditions underground. Wild fruits can be cultivated in all the same ways as regular wild fruits in the Overworld.",

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "- Lavacado - Magmango",

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath_wild_fruits.json",
          "text": "Temperature: 8 - 80 °C Hydration: 20 - 100 % Lavacado trees grow in the months of December through April, start flowering in May and June, and bear fruit in July.",

          "text": "If you see Tualang or Fig trees in your travels, you'll be pleased to know that you'll be able to create Boats with them just like any other kind of Wood",

          "text": "Deep within the nether you can utilize both Crimson and Warped wood to create some rather flashy Boats. Format Error: Due to a limitation, Watercrafts created with these materials are NOT impervious to lava, you WILL sink and BURN.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Softwoods have sufficiently soft enough pulp and bark that they're perfect for creating Dugout Canoes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "- Crimson",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "Known for their exceptional sturdiness, Hardwood trees are ideal for creating larger and sophisticated Watercrafts such as Rowboats and Sloops",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath",
          "source": "jar:firmacivplus-1.0.1-1.20.1.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/beneath.json",
          "text": "- Warped",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Beneath",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Products",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "The most simple glass products are Glass Panes and Glass Blocks. In order to craft them, you must start with a Blowpipe with a Glass Batch, and then perform a Pour. - Table Pours are used to create Glass Panes - Basin Pours are used to create Glass Blocks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass can also be dyed before it is poured to create colored glass. The color is dependent on the type of glass batch, and any powders that have been added. Each type of Glass Batch has a different natural color of glass that they will create. Silica glass batches can be made into many colors, Olivine, and Volcanic glass can be made into relatively few colors.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Panes are made with a Table Pour. A pouring table is made by placing up to sixteen Kaolin Clay Blocks or Brass Plated Blocks in a continuous area. 1. Add a Glass Batch to a Blowpipe. 2. Heat the blowpipe to Faint Red. 3. the Blowpipe on the top of the table. 4. Finally with a Paddle to flatten the glass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Table Pour",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Once the glass is cooled, it can be broken with a Gem Saw to obtain.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass Blocks are made with a Basin Pour. A basin is made by surrounding all sides of an air block except the top with Kaolin Clay Blocks or Brass Plated Blocks. 1. Add a Glass Batch to a Blowpipe. 2. Heat the blowpipe to Faint Red. 3. the Blowpipe on the top of the table.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Basin Pour",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Once the glass is cooled, it can be broken with a Gem Saw to obtain.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glassworking Blocks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Brass plated blocks can be created by holding a brass plate and on any stone brick.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Glass has a natural color based on the type of Glass Batch that was used. Other colors can be made using a Bowl. To use, place the Bowl on the ground, then the required Powder. Before Pouring, use the Blowpipe on the bowl to add the powder to the batch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Coloring Glass",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "- White: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Soda Ash - Black: Any Glass + Graphite - Gray: Any + Graphite + Soda Ash - Light Gray: Any + Graphite + 2x Soda Ash - Purple: Any + Iron + Copper - Brown: Any + Nickel - Cyan: Non-Volcanic Glass + Copper + Sapphire - Green: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Iron",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "Dye Colors",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.title",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "- Lime: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Iron + Soda Ash - Light Blue: Silica Glass + Lapis Lazuli - Blue: Silica Glass + Copper - Red: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Tin - Yellow: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Silver - Orange: Silica Glass + Pyrite - Magenta: Silica or Hematitic Glass + Ruby - Pink: Silica Glass + Gold - Tinted: Non-Silica Glass + Amethyst",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/glassworking_applications.json",
          "text": "The availability of glass colors. 'C' is clear, 'T' is tinted. Colors can only be crafted with certain glass batches.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Glass Products",
          "item_ref_count": 8,

        },

        },

    {
      "id": "tfc:beekeeping",
      "label": "Beekeeping",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 11,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "confidence": 0.7
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "Beehives are a place to house bees. Beehives need Beehive Frames inside them for the bees to live. Removing frames from an active hive will cause the bees to attack you, unless done at night, or with a Firepit underneath the hive active. Beehives can share flowers. The benefit of flowers diminishes after 60 flowers.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "Beehives know about the area in a 5 block radius from them. If there are at least 10 flowers around the hive, there is a chance an empty frame will be populated with a Queen. This is indicated by bee particles flying around the hive. Having 4 empty frames in a hive greatly increases the chances of bees moving in.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "If a beehive has two frames with queens, and an empty frame, the two colonies have a chance of Breeding and producing a new queen in the empty frame. This has the effect of passing on the Abilities of each parent to the offspring. Abilities are different traits bees have that change how they effect the world around them. They are on a scale of 1-10, with 10 being the max.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "Bees also produce Honey. Using with an Empty Jar on a hive that visibly has honey gives you a Honey Jar. Opening a Honey Jar gives you Raw Honey, a Sugar substitute.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "Pressing a filled frame in your inventory with a Knife gives you Beeswax, which has many uses. However, this kills the queen inside the frame so be careful!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "The most important use of beeswax is in creating Treated Lumber.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "- Bees can help fertilize planters! - Scraping a frame sacrifices the queen. Be smart! - Being wet prevents bees from attacking you.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "Bee Tips",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "- Hardiness: Allows bees to produce honey at lower temperatures. Hardiness 10 allows up to -16°C, whereas Hardiness 1 allows up to 2°C. - Production: Improves the speed of honey production. - Mutant: Increases variability in the traits passed during breeding.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "List of Abilities",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.title",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "- Fertility: Increases likelihood of breeding. - Crop Affinity: Likelihood of spreading a small amount of nutrients to crops. - Nature Restoration: Causes new flowers and lilypads to spawn around the hive. - Calmness: Decreases likelihood of bees attacking you.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/beekeeping.json",
          "text": "Bees with high Mutant ability have a chance of developing a Genetic Disease. Diseased bees pass on their disease to their offspring, and don't produce honey.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Beekeeping",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 11
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "firmalife:story/hive",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!data/firmalife/advancements/story/hive.json",
          "text": "So they just move in on their own?",
          "key": "advancement-title:title",
          "label": "So they just move in on their own?",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "advancement",
          "id": "firmalife:story/hive",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!data/firmalife/advancements/story/hive.json",
          "text": "Craft a beehive.",
          "key": "advancement-description:description",
          "label": "So they just move in on their own?",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "kubejs_tooltip",
          "id": "kubejs:tooltips/2",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/client_scripts/tooltips.js",
          "text": "Needs to be filled with empty frames and surrounded by flowers to attract bees.",
          "key": "kubejs-tooltip:tfg.tooltip.beehive",
          "label": "Needs to be filled with empty frames and surrounded by flowers to attract bees.",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "queststfc_tips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "This quest chapter gives you a lot, (and we do mean a lot) of tips on how to survive and thrive.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Each quest branch representing major mods in TerraFirmaGreg, with tips to make the most out of them. None of these quests are necessary for progression, although some may be locked behind main questline tasks.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Dear God that's a lot of branches...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Tips and Tricks be here!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Firmalife is a mod all about extending the agricultural and gastronomic experience in TerraFirmaCraft, with touches of miscellaneous features. Includes things such as decorations, more preservation methods, new foods, bees and a plethora of useful gadgets!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Thrive in TerraFirmaCraft",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "ArborFirmaCraft is a mod that adds multiple new trees to TFG: some are variants of existing TFC trees, such as Ancient Kapok, while others are completely new, such as the Baobab and Hevea. Some of the plank and log textures of these trees may remind you of vanilla tree colors...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "This section will go over Tree Tapping.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "As a bonus, all ArborFirmaCraft woods are compatible with FirmaLife, and Firma:Civ, we made sure of it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/tapping_index",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Harvesting the Life-blood of Trees",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Deep, DEEP underground, past the Bedrock, you'll find The Beneath, a complex network of huge caves filled with dangers and strange flora and fauna. While The Beneath is completely optional, it contains a lot of resources as well as some quality of life improvements.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:beneath/beneath",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Journey to the Center of the Earth",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The Beneath",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "TerraFirmaCraft is one of the main mods in TerraFirmaGreg. This branch mostly focuses on some of its survival features and other Add-Ons the modpack has, such as Aged Alcohol and Canes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The other two thirds of the modpack's name",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "TerraFirmaCraft Addons",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To begin tapping trees, you'll need to forge a Tree Tap from working Copper Ingots on an Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "The process of tapping requires finding a suitable tree, a dedicated Tapping Index in the ArborFirmaCraft Field Guide section will tell you how to find these trees. There's also the Tapping Trees entry, which can be used to learn how to actually tap them.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/tree_tapping",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Now with less jank!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Utilizing the Vacuum Chamber and some additional heating, you can process Latex from various trees into Raw Rubber Pulp through a specific process. You can then smelt together 3 Raw Rubber Pulp with a bit of Sulfur Dust in an Alloy Smelter to create Rubber Ingots, perfect for insulating cables and very much necessary to advance in the Voltaic Age and beyond...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: As you progress, there will be several ways to make this process cheaper and easier. Keep checking EMI! You'll be using Rubber for a long time, so it's well worth automating.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Insulate them cables",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Rubber Ingot",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Sticky Resin can be utilized as slime balls in some recipes. It is also key in the production of Resin Circuits and Resistors, which you'll need a fair bit of to advance into the Low Voltage age.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To make sticky resin, you need to boil either Latex or Conifer Pitch in a Vat mixed with Wood Ash.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "No, it's not Rosin",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Sticky Resin",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Latex Trees can be found in temperate areas of the world that receive large amounts of rainfall. It can be tapped to obtain Latex, used to create both Sticky Resin, and Vulcanized Latex. The latter is used to produce Rubber and Rubber Gloves, which protect you from poisonous materials on contact.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To complete this quest, you need to look at a block that has the tag #tfg:latex_logs. You can use EMI to learn which blocks have this tag by searching with that hashtag.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/making_rubber",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Seek for the Tropics",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfg:latex_logs)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Any #tfg:latex_logs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Latex Trees",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Syrup Trees can be found in temperate areas of the world that receive moderate amounts of rainfall. It can be tapped to obtain Syrup. Syrup can be used to create Tree Sugars, an alternative sugar among other uses.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To complete this quest, you need to look at a block that has the tag #tfg:syrup_logs. You can use EMI to learn which blocks have this tag.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:arborfirmacraft/making_syrups",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Diabetes is Skyrocketing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.lookat} #tfg:syrup_logs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "#tfg:syrup_logs",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Syrup Trees",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Tree Sugar is a sugar substitute, mostly used as a food ingredient.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "To make it, you'll need to boil Tree Sap to get Concentrated Sap, then boil it again to get Syrup. Finally, use a workbench to turn a Bucket of Syrup into Tree sugar.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfc_tips",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfc_tips.snbt",
          "text": "Keep in mind that all these boiling processes require a stick in the Vat or pot.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "queststfc_tips",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },

          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/bread.json",
          "text": "To make Bread, one first must get Yeast. To get your first yeast, seal Dried Fruit in a Barrel of Water. After three days, Yeast Starter will form. From now on, your yeast can be fed by sealing Yeast Starter in a Barrel with Flour. This causes it to multiply. 1 flour per 100mB of Yeast produces 600mB of Yeast. That's a good deal!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Bread",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/bread",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/bread.json",
          "text": "Yeast Starter, Sweetener, and Flour can be combined to make Dough. Dough can be cooked like normal to produce Bread!.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Bread",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/bread",

          "text": "Sliced Bread",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Bread",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/bread",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/bread.json",
          "text": "Once baked, you can use a knife to cut bread into slices. These can then either be used for sandwich making, or cooked into toast which can be spread with butter or preserves.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Bread",
          "item_ref_count": 5,

          "count": 4
        },
